# PROGRESS.md - ALVILLAS OS

Format: one line per handbook feature ID as `F-ID | TODO | IN-PROGRESS | DONE <PR/commit evidence>`.

Archived checklist evidence remains in `docs/archive/`; it is not converted to feature `DONE` status unless the handbook Definition of Done is verified.

F-FND-06 | DONE | MFA enrollment retry PR #52 https://github.com/NadLag/The.alvillas.OS/pull/52 merged c72046747ec5118221dc42627ce574eafb447320. PR CI https://github.com/NadLag/The.alvillas.OS/actions/runs/38624863767 and main CI https://github.com/NadLag/The.alvillas.OS/actions/runs/38625672566 passed full checks, including 12741 unit tests, 25 PostgreSQL integration tests, schema drift, restore, and 52 desktop/mobile Playwright tests. Full local checks also passed; local Playwright used zero retries. Rejected authenticator codes now show EN/FR/AR guidance and retain the same pending enrollment; setup and confirmation controls stay disabled while requests are pending. Staging deployment dpl_k7u22g8MoXFk7bj6gTnZyVJZ8ya7 on the existing independent project prj_GJBdVD7r7fBkG7vH9rn2K2eLwobC is READY in preview; deployment metadata and /api/version match c72046747ec5118221dc42627ce574eafb447320 with ref main. Fresh protected Playwright passed both landing Sign In actions, recovery navigation, EN/FR/AR RTL, desktop/360px mobile, keyboard and Phone + PIN form switching. The staging administrator password was recovered through the existing one-time recovery mechanism with MFA still required; identity, roles, organization and all original protected business rows were preserved. One owner-created invitation and person record were retained; comparison of those two tables with the restored snapshot found no original person or invitation rows changed or removed. Expired and unconfirmed MFA setup attempts and all issued temporary administrator recovery codes/sessions/devices were revoked. The owner enrolled their own authenticator through the staging security panel and confirmed that the eight new recovery codes were saved securely. A fresh administrator Email + Password -> owner TOTP -> Dashboard -> Logout flow passed on the permanent staging URL, with secure HttpOnly cookies, session-cookie removal, protected-page redirect after logout and an unauthenticated session API response. Outdated staging MFA factors were retired only after confirmed owner enrollment and recovery-code storage; MFA remained required. All temporary recovery credentials, sessions and devices were revoked. A later administrator organization-settings save changed the brand colors and default language from FR to EN; the owner explicitly requested keeping those staging settings. Comparison with the restored snapshot found only those approved profile preferences and update timestamps changed in organization/profile records, with no organization records added or removed and all other original administrator, roles, organization and business fields preserved. Independent staging verification passed temporary field-account activation, Email + Password after recovery, Phone + PIN, dashboard and logout in FR at 360px. Two labeled temporary test accounts were deactivated with credentials, sessions and devices revoked; test identity history was retained and all original administrator/roles/organization/business data were preserved. No production deployment, credentials, database changes or migrations were performed. Exact automation blocker: GitHub staging VERCEL_ORG_ID, VERCEL_PROJECT_ID and VERCEL_TOKEN are missing; CI skips staging deployment steps. Scope unchanged: all 355 features remain required. 2026-10-23: Main CI 39572179526 recovered green after rerunning its failed staging deployment job; exact bed60021a62713acd311ab43f48051ad6bd343cf verified at the stable Preview alias. Initial immediate alias verification had read the prior SHA during propagation. Field queue increment also pins Next.js 16.4.3 after npm flagged 16.4.0 security advisories; npm audit now has zero high/critical findings (three pre-existing low findings remain). Signed Inngest discovery confirms six functions; unsigned/tampered discovery and legacy unsigned drain remain denied. F-IDN-03 | DONE | 2026-10-24 accepted on exact main Preview ccbb1fc7208f38ee597119d99d94e407a396de84: PR #91 https://github.com/NadLag/The.alvillas.OS/pull/91 and green main CI https://github.com/NadLag/The.alvillas.OS/actions/runs/40090320185 passed 14,711 unit, 144 real PostgreSQL and 144 zero-retry desktop/mobile browser tests. At the owner's explicit request, a dedicated staging test account was created through Identity invitation UI, scoped to one new acceptance job. The employee chose its own masked PIN through native activation with no password credential; the invitation could not be reused. staging-field-pin-workflow.private.json records 18 EN/FR/Arabic RTL desktop/mobile activation, login and assigned-task layouts with keyboard, axe and overflow checks; three native PIN login audits; three authorized test-account PIN reset audits; one active Argon2id PIN, three revoked PINs, old-PIN rejection, denied revoked session, zero foreign-tenant rows and only task:view/task:edit for that job. Other-worker task access and forged actor/tenant pulls were denied. PINs existed only in memory and were never persisted or displayed. The original verifier incorrectly required UUIDs in the expected blank-ID revoked-session response; its failed checkpoint is retained. Read-only recovery proved the first reset committed before two fresh, separately checkpointed test-account rotations verified denial and new-PIN login. Administrator credentials/MFA were untouched. Existing PostgreSQL and browser tests cover configured persistent cross-IP PIN backoff and rate limits. No production changes. All 355 features remain required.
